Key requirements:
- ONE HTML file, load three.min.js r128 from cdnjs.
- Minecraft-style voxel game.
- Blocks IDs colors.
- Chunk 16x16 columns, 80 high. Global Map chunks key "cx,cz" value {data Uint8Array, mesh?}. Helpers read/write block global.
- Each frame: generate data for chunks within 5 chunks max 4; build meshes for chunks within 4 whose 4 neighbors have data max 2; for chunks farther than 7 remove mesh dispose geometry delete entry.
- Meshing one BufferGeometry per chunk. Use world coords vertices, mesh at origin. Global array chunkMeshes for raycasting. Rebuild function.
- Terrain deterministic value noise hash, smoothed 2D, 4 octaves fractal, 3D caves. Height formula. Column fill. Trees.
- Player controls: pointer lock, camera rotation YXZ, yaw/pitch, mouse look sensitivity 0.002 clamp pitch. Player box half-width 0.3 height 1.8 eye 1.62 spawn x=8 z=8 above terrain. Gravity 25, jump velocity 8.5, WASD 5.5. Axis-separated collision. Falling below -20 respawn.
- Break/place: crosshair, raycast center max 6. hit point p and normal n: break target floor(p - n*0.5), place floor(p + n*0.5). Outline wireframe. Only locked: left click air not y0; right place selected if air and no overlap player. Rebuild chunk and neighbor if border. Context menu suppressed.
- Hotbar 7 slots colors numbers selected white border. Keys 1-7 wheel wraps.
- Sky 0x87ceeb fog 40-110, ambient 0.65 directional 0.8. ~25 flat white transparent boxes clouds height ~90 drifting wrap around player. Water plane at height 14.3 semi-transparent blue re-centered.
- UI start overlay controls, click to play, reappears on pointerlockrelease. Body no margins fullscreen canvas resize.
- Avoid issues.

Noise:
hash2(x,y): integer hash to 0-1. Use Math.imul? Need deterministic. Could use:
function hashInt(x, y) { let n = x * 374761393 + y * 668265263; n = (n ^ (n >> 13)) * 1274126177; n = n ^ (n >> 16); return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295; }
But JS bitwise 32-bit. Multiplication large may overflow to double; use Math.imul to keep 32-bit. e.g. let n = Math.imul(x, 374761393) + Math.imul(y, 668265263); n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177); n ^= n >>> 16; return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295.
Need hash3 for caves: combine z.
function hash3(x,y,z) { let n = Math.imul(x, 374761393) + Math.imul(y, 668265263) + Math.imul(z, 1274126177); n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177); n ^= n >>> 16; return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295; }
Need integer coordinates can be negative; Math.imul handles.
noise2(x,y): floor, frac, smoothstep, bilinear of hash2 at integer corners.
noise3 similar.
fractal2(x,y): 4 octaves: amp 1, freq 1; total amp 1.5? Need output 0-1 maybe. Use amp=0.5, total=0.9375? Or normalize.
function fractal2(x,y){ let value=0, amp=0.5, freq=1, max=0; for i 0..3 { value += noise2(x*freq,y*freq)*amp; max += amp; amp*=0.5; freq*=2; } return value/max; }
3D similar.
Height: m = fractal2(x*0.004,z*0.004), h=fractal2(x*0.02,z*0.02), H=floor(5 + m*m*58 + h*10). Max 5+58+10=73, within 80. Min 5.

Frame updateWorld implementation:
function updateWorld(dt){
 const pcx=Math.floor(player.x/CHUNK), pcz=Math.floor(player.z/CHUNK);
 // generate
 let genCount=0;
 for(let r=0;r<=5 && genCount<4;r++){
   for(let dx=-r; dx<=r && genCount<4; dx++){
     for(let dz=-r; dz<=r && genCount<4; dz++){
       if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dz)) !== r) continue; // ring
       // But r=0 includes center. Need ensure all within 5. This generates rings.
       if(!chunks.has((pcx+dx)+','+(pcz+dz))){ ensureChunkData(pcx+dx,pcz+dz); genCount++; }
     }
   }
 }
 // build
 let buildCount=0;
 for(let r=0;r<=4 && buildCount<2;r++){
   for(let dx=-r; dx<=r && buildCount<2; dx++){
     for(let dz=-r; dz<=r && buildCount<2; dz++){
       if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dz)) !== r) continue;
       const cx=pcx+dx,cz=pcz+dz;
       const chunk=chunks.get(cx+','+cz);
       if(chunk && !chunk.mesh && neighborsHaveData(cx,cz)){ buildChunkMesh(cx,cz); buildCount++; }
     }
   }
 }
 // cleanup
 const toDelete=[];
 for(const [key,chunk] of chunks){
   const parts=key.split(','); const cx=parseInt(parts[0]), cz=parseInt(parts[1]);
   if(Math.max(Math.abs(cx-pcx),Math.abs(cz-pcz)) > 7){ removeChunkMesh(chunk); toDelete.push(key); }
 }
 for(const key of toDelete) chunks.delete(key);
}
But ring loops: for r=0, condition Math.max(0,0)!==0 false? Actually if !== r continue; for r=0, dx=0,dz=0, max=0, not continue. Good. For r=1, includes all with max=1 (8 neighbors). Good.
But generating all within 5: ring by ring. Good.
Build ring similarly. But if a chunk at r=0 missing mesh and neighbors have data, build. If center mesh exists, skip. If moving, new chunks at r=5 generated, then build at r=4 later. Good.

If chunk empty, neighbors have data, buildChunkMesh creates no mesh, chunk.mesh remains null, so every frame it will try build again (max2). Could be okay but repeated. Could set chunk.mesh = false? But spec value plus mesh. Could set chunk.built=true. But empty chunks rare (air above? chunks always have terrain). If all air? Not possible due y0. So okay.

function neighborsHaveData(cx,cz){ return chunks.has((cx+1)+','+cz) && chunks.has((cx-1)+','+cz) && chunks.has(cx+','+(cz+1)) && chunks.has(cx+','+(cz-1)); }

Collision details:
function collides(x,y,z){
 const minX=Math.floor(x-0.3), maxX=Math.floor(x+0.3);
 const minY=Math.floor(y), maxY=Math.floor(y+1.8);
 const minZ=Math.floor(z-0.3), maxZ=Math.floor(z+0.3);
 for(let bx=minX; bx<=maxX; bx++) for(let by=minY; by<=maxY; by++) for(let bz=minZ; bz<=maxZ; bz++) if(readBlock(bx,by,bz)!==0) return true;
 return false;
}
If player feet y=H+1, minY=H+1 (air), maxY=floor(H+2.8)=H+2 (air). No collision. If feet y=H+0.999, minY=H (solid) collision. Good.
If player jumps, maxY may hit ceiling. If collides y positive, revert and velocity.y=0.
Need if player.x+0.3 exactly integer, floor includes block at that integer? AABB [x-0.3,x+0.3] includes boundary. If x+0.3=1.0, floor=1, readBlock(1) may collide though block spans 1..2 and player touches face. Minecraft prevents overlapping but touching okay. Our collides will treat touch as collision, may cause sticking. Could shrink epsilon. Use minX=Math.floor(x-0.3+1e-6), maxX=Math.floor(x+0.3-1e-6)? But if exactly boundary, should not collide. Use EPS=1e-6: minX=Math.floor(x-0.3+EPS), maxX=Math.floor(x+0.3-EPS). Similarly y. But if y=0, minY=floor(EPS)=0. Good. maxY=floor(y+1.8-EPS). If y+1.8 integer, exclude block above. Good. Use EPS.
But if player exactly at block edge x=0.3, minx=floor(0+eps)=0, block spans 0..1, player spans 0..0.6? Actually x=0.3, minx=0, collides if block at 0. Good. If x=0.3000001, okay. If x=0.7, max=floor(1.0-eps)=0, not include block 1, touching. Good.

Move function:
function updatePlayer(dt){
 // input
 const speed=5.5;
 let moveF=0, moveR=0;
 if(keys['KeyW']) moveF++; if(keys['KeyS']) moveF--; if(keys['KeyD']) moveR++; if(keys['KeyA']) moveR--;
 const sin=Math.sin(yaw), cos=Math.cos(yaw);
 let vx = (-sin*moveF + cos*moveR);
 let vz = (-cos*moveF - sin*moveR); // right? Let's derive: forward=(-sin,-cos), right=(cos,-sin). So x=-sin*F+cos*R, z=-cos*F - sin*R. Good.
 const len=Math.hypot(vx,vz); if(len>0){ vx/=len; vz/=len; }
 vx*=speed; vz*=speed;
 if(keys['Space'] && onGround){ velocity.y=8.5; onGround=false; }
 velocity.y -= 25*dt;
 if(velocity.y < -50) velocity.y=-50;
 // move x
 player.x += vx*dt;
 if(collides(player.x,player.y,player.z)) player.x -= vx*dt;
 // move z
 player.z += vz*dt;

   player.y=oldY;
   if(velocity.y < 0) onGround=true;
   velocity.y=0;
 } else {
   if(velocity.y < 0) onGround=false; // but if not moving down? okay
 }

}

Hash function quality:
function hash2(x,y){
 let n = Math.imul(x, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y, 668265263);
 n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177);
 n ^= n >>> 16;
 return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}
Using ^ not +? okay. Need ensure negative x,y. Math.imul negative returns signed 32-bit. n ^ ... okay. n>>>0 unsigned. Good.

noise3:
function noise3(x,y,z){
 const x0=Math.floor(x), y0=..., z0=...;
 const xf=x-x0, yf=y-y0, zf=z-z0;
 const u=smooth(xf),v=smooth(yf),w=smooth(zf);
 const c000=hash3(x0,y0,z0), c100=hash3(x0+1,y0,z0), c010=hash3(x0,y0+1,z0), c110=hash3(x0+1,y0+1,z0), c001=hash3(x0,y0,z0+1), c101=hash3(x0+1,y0,z0+1), c011=hash3(x0,y0+1,z0+1), c111=hash3(x0+1,y0+1,z0+1);
 const x00=lerp(c000,c100,u), x10=lerp(c010,c110,u), x01=lerp(c001,c101,u), x11=lerp(c011,c111,u);
 const y0=lerp(x00,x10,v), y1=lerp(x01,x11,v);
 return lerp(y0,y1,w);
}

let val=0,amp=0.5,freq=1,max=0; for 4 { val += noise3(x*freq,y*freq,z*freq)*amp; max+=amp; amp*=0.5; freq*=2; } return val/max;

Hash functions:
function smooth(t){return t*t*(3-2*t);}
function lerp(a,b,t){return a+(b-a)*t;}
function hash2(x,y){ let n = Math.imul(x, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y, 668265263); n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177); n ^= n >>> 16; return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295; }
function hash3(x,y,z){ let n = Math.imul(x, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y, 668265263) ^ Math.imul(z, 1274126177); n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177); n ^= n >>> 16; return (n >>> 0) / 4294967295; }

terrainHeight:
function terrainHeight(wx,wz){ let m=fractal2(wx*0.004,wz*0.004); let h=fractal2(wx*0.02,wz*0.02); let H=Math.floor(5 + m*m*58 + h*10); if(H<0)H=0; if(H>78)H=78; return H; }

Chunk key:
function chunkKey(cx,cz){ return cx+','+cz; }

idx:
function blockIndex(x,y,z){ return (x*CHUNK_SIZE + z)*CHUNK_HEIGHT + y; }

ensureChunkData:
function ensureChunkData(cx,cz){ const key=chunkKey(cx,cz); let chunk=chunks.get(key); if(chunk) return chunk; chunk={data:new Uint8Array(CHUNK_SIZE*CHUNK_SIZE*CHUNK_HEIGHT), mesh:null}; chunks.set(key,chunk); generateChunkData(chunk,cx,cz); return chunk; }

generateChunkData:
function generateChunkData(chunk,cx,cz){ const data=chunk.data; for(let lx=0;lx<CHUNK_SIZE;lx++){ for(let lz=0;lz<CHUNK_SIZE;lz++){ const wx=cx*CHUNK_SIZE+lx; const wz=cz*CHUNK_SIZE+lz; const H=terrainHeight(wx,wz); for(let y=0;y<=H;y++){ let id; if(y===0) id=3; else if(y < H-3) id=3; else if(y < H){ if(H<=16) id=4; else if(H>=37) id=3; else id=2; } else { if(H>=46) id=7; else if(H>=37) id=3; else if(H<=16) id=4; else id=1; } if(y>0 && y>=3 && y<=H-2 && fractal3(wx*0.09,y*0.09,wz*0.09) > 0.67) id=0; data[blockIndex(lx,y,lz)]=id; } } }
 // trees second pass
 for(let lx=0; lx<CHUNK_SIZE; lx++){ for(let lz=0; lz<CHUNK_SIZE; lz++){ const wx=cx*CHUNK_SIZE+lx; const wz=cz*CHUNK_SIZE+lz; const H=terrainHeight(wx,wz); if(H>=17 && H<37? Actually surface grass condition H between 17 and 36. But can just check data at surface == 1. if(blockIndex(lx,H,lz) out? H<=78. if(data[blockIndex(lx,H,lz)]!==1) continue; if(hash2(wx*3+7, wz*5+13) >= 0.02) continue; if(Math.abs(wx-8)<=2 && Math.abs(wz-8)<=2) continue; if(lx<2 || lx>13 || lz<2 || lz>13) continue; // trunk fits and leaves inside
 // trunk
 for(let dy=1; dy<=4; dy++){ const y=H+dy; if(y>=CHUNK_HEIGHT) break; const i=blockIndex(lx,y,lz); if(data[i]===0) data[i]=5; }

 } }
}

readBlock/writeBlock:
function readBlock(wx,wy,wz){ if(wy<0 || wy>=CHUNK_HEIGHT) return 0; const cx=Math.floor(wx/CHUNK_SIZE); const cz=Math.floor(wz/CHUNK_SIZE); const chunk=chunks.get(chunkKey(cx,cz)); if(!chunk) return 0; const lx=wx-cx*CHUNK_SIZE; const lz=wz-cz*CHUNK_SIZE; return chunk.data[blockIndex(lx,wy,lz)]; }
function writeBlock(wx,wy,wz,id){ if(wy<0||wy>=CHUNK_HEIGHT) return false; const cx=Math.floor(wx/CHUNK_SIZE); const cz=Math.floor(wz/CHUNK_SIZE); const chunk=chunks.get(chunkKey(cx,cz)); if(!chunk) return false; const lx=wx-cx*CHUNK_SIZE; const lz=wz-cz*CHUNK_SIZE; chunk.data[blockIndex(lx,wy,lz)]=id; return true; }

removeChunkMesh:
function removeChunkMesh(chunk){ if(chunk.mesh){ scene.remove(chunk.mesh); chunk.mesh.geometry.dispose(); const idx=chunkMeshes.indexOf(chunk.mesh); if(idx>=0) chunkMeshes.splice(idx,1); chunk.mesh=null; } }

buildChunkMesh:
function buildChunkMesh(cx,cz){ const key=chunkKey(cx,cz); const chunk=chunks.get(key); if(!chunk) return; removeChunkMesh(chunk); const positions=[]; const normals=[]; const colors=[]; const baseX=cx*CHUNK_SIZE; const baseZ=cz*CHUNK_SIZE; for(let lx=0; lx<CHUNK_SIZE; lx++){ for(let lz=0; lz<CHUNK_SIZE; lz++){ for(let y=0; y<CHUNK_HEIGHT; y++){ const id=chunk.data[blockIndex(lx,y,lz)]; if(id===0) continue; const wx=baseX+lx; const wz=baseZ+lz; const rgb=blockRGB[id]; for(let f=0; f<6; f++){ const face=FACES[f]; const nx=wx+face.dir[0]; const ny=y+face.dir[1]; const nz=wz+face.dir[2]; if(readBlock(nx,ny,nz)!==0) continue; const shade=face.shade; const r=rgb[0]*shade, g=rgb[1]*shade, b=rgb[2]*shade; const v=face.verts; addTriangle... } } } } if(positions.length===0) return; const geometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry(); geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions,3)); geometry.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(normals,3)); geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(colors,3)); const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geometry, blockMaterial); scene.add(mesh); chunk.mesh=mesh; chunkMeshes.push(mesh); }

neighborsHaveData:
function neighborsHaveData(cx,cz){ return chunks.has(chunkKey(cx+1,cz)) && chunks.has(chunkKey(cx-1,cz)) && chunks.has(chunkKey(cx,cz+1)) && chunks.has(chunkKey(cx,cz-1)); }

updateWorld:
function updateWorld(){ // no dt needed except clouds? clouds separate
 const pcx=Math.floor(player.x/CHUNK_SIZE); const pcz=Math.floor(player.z/CHUNK_SIZE);
 let gen=0;
 for(let r=0; r<=5 && gen<4; r++){
   for(let dx=-r; dx<=r && gen<4; dx++){
     for(let dz=-r; dz<=r && gen<4; dz++){
       if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dz)) !== r) continue;
       const cx=pcx+dx, cz=pcz+dz;
       if(!chunks.has(chunkKey(cx,cz))){ ensureChunkData(cx,cz); gen++; }
     }
   }
 }
 let built=0;
 for(let r=0; r<=4 && built<2; r++){
   for(let dx=-r; dx<=r && built<2; dx++){
     for(let dz=-r; dz<=r && built<2; dz++){
       if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dz)) !== r) continue;
       const cx=pcx+dx, cz=pcz+dz;
       const chunk=chunks.get(chunkKey(cx,cz));
       if(chunk && !chunk.mesh && neighborsHaveData(cx,cz)){ buildChunkMesh(cx,cz); built++; }
     }
   }
 }

 for(const [key,chunk] of chunks){ const parts=key.split(','); const cx=parseInt(parts[0],10); const cz=parseInt(parts[1],10); if(Math.max(Math.abs(cx-pcx), Math.abs(cz-pcz)) > 7){ removeChunkMesh(chunk); remove.push(key); } }
 for(const key of remove) chunks.delete(key);
}

Now updatePlayer with substeps:
function moveAxis(axis, amount){ if(amount===0) return; const steps=Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.abs(amount)/0.5)); const step=amount/steps; for(let i=0;i<steps;i++){ const old=player[axis]; player[axis]+=step; if(collides(player.x,player.y,player.z)){ player[axis]=old; if(axis==='y'){ if(amount<0) onGround=true; velocity.y=0; } return; } } if(axis==='y' && amount<0) onGround=false; }

collides:
function collides(x,y,z){ const eps=0.0001; const minX=Math.floor(x-0.3+eps); const maxX=Math.floor(x+0.3-eps); const minY=Math.floor(y+eps); const maxY=Math.floor(y+1.8-eps); const minZ=Math.floor(z-0.3+eps); const maxZ=Math.floor(z+0.3-eps); for(let bx=minX; bx<=maxX; bx++){ for(let by=minY; by<=maxY; by++){ for(let bz=minZ; bz<=maxZ; bz++){ if(readBlock(bx,by,bz)!==0) return true; } } } return false; }
If y negative? minY floor negative. readBlock y<0 returns air. Good.
If y+1.8-eps < y? no.

updatePlayer:
function updatePlayer(dt){
 let mf=0,mr=0;
 if(keys['KeyW']) mf++; if(keys['KeyS']) mf--; if(keys['KeyD']) mr++; if(keys['KeyA']) mr--;
 const sin=Math.sin(yaw), cos=Math.cos(yaw);
 let vx=-sin*mf + cos*mr;
 let vz=-cos*mf - sin*mr;
 const len=Math.sqrt(vx*vx+vz*vz);
 if(len>0){ vx=vx/len*5.5; vz=vz/len*5.5; }
 if(keys['Space'] && onGround){ velocity.y=8.5; onGround=false; }
 velocity.y -= 25*dt;
 if(velocity.y < -50) velocity.y=-50;
 onGround=false;
 moveAxis('x', vx*dt);
 moveAxis('z', vz*dt);
 moveAxis('y', velocity.y*dt);
 if(player.y < -20) respawn();
 camera.position.set(player.x, player.y+1.62, player.z);
 camera.rotation.set(pitch, yaw, 0);
}
Need velocity.x/z not stored; okay.
If no input, vx=0.

respawn:
function respawn(){ player.x=8; player.z=8; player.y=terrainHeight(8,8)+6; velocity.y=0; onGround=false; }

updateTarget:
function updateTarget(){
 if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement){ target.valid=false; outline.visible=false; return; }
 raycaster.setFromCamera(center, camera);
 const intersects=raycaster.intersectObjects(chunkMeshes, false);
 if(intersects.length>0){ const hit=intersects[0]; const p=hit.point; const n=hit.face.normal; target.valid=true; target.bx=Math.floor(p.x - n.x*0.5); target.by=Math.floor(p.y - n.y*0.5); target.bz=Math.floor(p.z - n.z*0.5); target.px=Math.floor(p.x + n.x*0.5); target.py=Math.floor(p.y + n.y*0.5); target.pz=Math.floor(p.z + n.z*0.5); outline.position.set(target.bx+0.5, target.by+0.5, target.bz+0.5); outline.visible=true; } else { target.valid=false; outline.visible=false; }
}
Need if hit.face.normal maybe not normalized due to non-uniform? Our normals unit. Good.

breakBlock:
function breakBlock(){ if(!target.valid) return; if(target.by===0) return; if(readBlock(target.bx,target.by,target.bz)===0) return; writeBlock(target.bx,target.by,target.bz,0); rebuildAround(target.bx,target.bz); }
placeBlock:
function placeBlock(){ if(!target.valid) return; const x=target.px,y=target.py,z=target.pz; if(y<0||y>=CHUNK_HEIGHT) return; if(readBlock(x,y,z)!==0) return; if(playerOverlapsCell(x,y,z)) return; writeBlock(x,y,z,selected+1); rebuildAround(x,z); }
playerOverlapsCell:
function playerOverlapsCell(bx,by,bz){ const eps=0.0001; const minx=player.x-0.3+eps, maxx=player.x+0.3-eps; const miny=player.y+eps, maxy=player.y+1.8-eps; const minz=player.z-0.3+eps, maxz=player.z+0.3-eps; return bx < maxx && bx+1 > minx && by < maxy && by+1 > miny && bz < maxz && bz+1 > minz; }
If player exactly adjacent, eps prevents overlap. Good.

rebuildAround:
function rebuildAround(wx,wz){ const cx=Math.floor(wx/CHUNK_SIZE), cz=Math.floor(wz/CHUNK_SIZE); rebuildChunk(cx,cz); const lx=wx-cx*CHUNK_SIZE, lz=wz-cz*CHUNK_SIZE; if(lx===0) rebuildChunk(cx-1,cz); if(lx===CHUNK_SIZE-1) rebuildChunk(cx+1,cz); if(lz===0) rebuildChunk(cx,cz-1); if(lz===CHUNK_SIZE-1) rebuildChunk(cx,cz+1); }
function rebuildChunk(cx,cz){ const chunk=chunks.get(chunkKey(cx,cz)); if(chunk) buildChunkMesh(cx,cz); }
If neighbor missing, skip. Good.

function createHotbar(){ const bar=document.getElementById('hotbar'); for(let i=0;i<7;i++){ const slot=document.createElement('div'); slot.className='slot'; slot.dataset.index=i; const color=BLOCK_COLORS[i+1]; slot.style.background='#'+color.toString(16).padStart(6,'0'); const span=document.createElement('span'); span.textContent=i+1; slot.appendChild(span); bar.appendChild(slot); } updateHotbar(); }
function updateHotbar(){ const slots=document.querySelectorAll('#hotbar .slot'); slots.forEach((s,i)=>{ s.classList.toggle('selected', i===selected); }); }
selectSlot(i){ selected=(i+7)%7; updateHotbar(); }

Events:
function initEvents(){
 window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
 window.addEventListener('contextmenu', e=>e.preventDefault());
 const overlay=document.getElementById('overlay');
 overlay.addEventListener('click', ()=>{ renderer.domElement.requestPointerLock(); });
 document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', ()=>{ if(document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement){ overlay.classList.add('hidden'); } else { overlay.classList.remove('hidden'); for(const k in keys) keys[k]=false; } });
 document.addEventListener('pointerlockerror', ()=>{ overlay.classList.remove('hidden'); });
 document.addEventListener('mousemove', e=>{ if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement) return; yaw -= e.movementX*0.002; pitch -= e.movementY*0.002; const lim=Math.PI/2-0.01; if(pitch>lim)pitch=lim; if(pitch<-lim)pitch=-lim; });
 window.addEventListener('mousedown', e=>{ if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement) return; e.preventDefault(); if(e.button===0) breakBlock(); else if(e.button===2) placeBlock(); });
 window.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{ keys[e.code]=true; if(e.code==='Space') e.preventDefault(); if(e.code.startsWith('Digit')){ const n=parseInt(e.code.slice(5),10); if(n>=1 && n<=7) selectSlot(n-1); } });
 window.addEventListener('keyup', e=>{ keys[e.code]=false; });
 window.addEventListener('blur', ()=>{ for(const k in keys) keys[k]=false; });
 window.addEventListener('wheel', e=>{ if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement) return; e.preventDefault(); if(e.deltaY>0) selected=(selected+1)%7; else selected=(selected+6)%7; updateHotbar(); }, {passive:false});
}

init:
function init(){
 scene=new THREE.Scene(); scene.background=new THREE.Color(0x87ceeb); scene.fog=new THREE.Fog(0x87ceeb,40,110);
 camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, window.innerWidth/window.innerHeight, 0.1, 400); camera.rotation.order='YXZ'; scene.add(camera);
 renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true}); renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio,2)); renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight); document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
 raycaster=new THREE.Raycaster(); raycaster.far=6;
 blockMaterial=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({vertexColors:true});
 const ambient=new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff,0.65); scene.add(ambient);
 const dir=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,0.8); dir.position.set(0.5,1,0.35); scene.add(dir);
 outline=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.002,1.002,1.002), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x000000, wireframe:true, fog:false})); outline.visible=false; scene.add(outline);
 // water
 const waterGeo=new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1000,1000); waterGeo.rotateX(-Math.PI/2); waterPlane=new THREE.Mesh(waterGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x2277ff, transparent:true, opacity:0.55, depthWrite:false, side:THREE.DoubleSide, fog:true})); waterPlane.position.set(player.x,14.3,player.z); waterPlane.renderOrder=1; scene.add(waterPlane);
 // clouds
 createClouds();
 // player spawn
 player.x=8; player.z=8; player.y=terrainHeight(8,8)+6; camera.position.set(player.x, player.y+1.62, player.z); camera.rotation.set(pitch,yaw,0);
 createHotbar();
 initEvents();
 // warmup
 const pcx=Math.floor(player.x/CHUNK_SIZE), pcz=Math.floor(player.z/CHUNK_SIZE);
 for(let dx=-2;dx<=2;dx++) for(let dz=-2;dz<=2;dz++) ensureChunkData(pcx+dx,pcz+dz);
 for(let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++) for(let dz=-1;dz<=1;dz++) if(neighborsHaveData(pcx+dx,pcz+dz)) buildChunkMesh(pcx+dx,pcz+dz);
 last=performance.now(); animate();
}

createClouds:
function createClouds(){ const mat=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffffff, transparent:true, opacity:0.75, depthWrite:false, fog:false}); for(let i=0;i<25;i++){ const x=(hash2(i*12345+1, i*67890+2)-0.5)*300; const z=(hash2(i*12345+3, i*67890+4)-0.5)*300; const y=88 + hash2(i*12345+5, i*67890+6)*8; const w=8 + hash2(i*12345+7, i*67890+8)*24; const d=8 + hash2(i*12345+9, i*67890+10)*24; const geo=new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,1,d); const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); mesh.position.set(x,y,z); mesh.renderOrder=2; scene.add(mesh); clouds.push(mesh); } }
updateClouds(dt):
function updateClouds(dt){ for(const cloud of clouds){ cloud.position.x += 1.2*dt; if(cloud.position.x > player.x+160) cloud.position.x -= 320; if(cloud.position.x < player.x-160) cloud.position.x += 320; if(cloud.position.z > player.z+160) cloud.position.z -= 320; if(cloud.position.z < player.z-160) cloud.position.z += 320; } }
Cloud material shared; geometry separate. Good.

Now, let's think about spec details and possible mismatches:
- "Chunks are 16x16 columns, 80 blocks high. Store them in a global Map (key "cx,cz", value: a Uint8Array of block IDs plus its mesh)." Our value object with data, mesh, built. Acceptable? It says value: a Uint8Array plus mesh. We use object. Fine.
- "Write global read/write block helpers using chunk = floor(world/16) and local = world − chunk*16". Good.
- "Each frame: generate block data for all chunks within 5 chunks of the player (max 4 per frame); build meshes for chunks within 4 chunks whose 4 neighbors already have data (max 2 per frame); for chunks farther than 7, remove the mesh, dispose its geometry and delete the entry." Our updateWorld does ring order max4/2. Good.
- "Use world coordinates for vertices and keep the mesh at the origin." Good.
- "Keep all chunk meshes in a global array for raycasting, and write a rebuild function for a chunk." Good.
- Terrain: "m = fractal at coords*0.004, h = fractal at coords*0.02, H = floor(5 + m*m*58 + h*10)." Good.
- "Column fill: y 0 = unbreakable stone; below H−3 stone; the 3 layers under the surface dirt (sand if H ≤ 16, stone if H ≥ 37); surface = snow if H ≥ 46, stone if H ≥ 37, sand if H ≤ 16, otherwise grass." Our interpretation surface at y=H. Need ensure "below H−3 stone" if H=5, y<2 stone, okay. But if they expected surface y=H-1, our terrain one block higher. Does it matter? Water plane 14.3 maybe. But spec likely ambiguous. Could adjust to common surface y=H-1 to match H as height. Let's re-evaluate. If H = floor(...) as height, typical fill y < H. Surface y=H-1. "below H−3 stone" means y < H-3? Then 3 layers under surface? If surface y=H-1, under surface y=H-2,H-3,H-4. "below H-3" would y < H-3 (0..H-4) stone, and 3 layers under surface y=H-3,H-2,H-1? includes surface? Not coherent. If they meant below H-3 (y < H-3) stone, the 3 layers under the surface (y=H-3,H-2,H-1) dirt/sand/stone, surface = y=H? Then H is surface y. So our interpretation likely intended. Good.
- "Caves: for y between 3 and H−2, if 3D noise at position*0.09 exceeds 0.67, set air." Our loop y>=3 && y<=H-2. Good.
- "Trees: on grass, when a per-column hash < 0.02 and the trunk fits inside the chunk: 4 wood blocks up, leaves as a 5x5 layer twice, then 3x3, then 1 on top (only into air)." We do. "trunk fits inside chunk" could mean trunk only, not leaves. But we restrict leaves inside to avoid cross-chunk. Good.
- "PerspectiveCamera (fov 75, far 400), rotation order "YXZ" driven by yaw/pitch." Good.

Corners:
c000 = hash3(x0,y0,z0)
c100 = hash3(x0+1,y0,z0)
c010 = hash3(x0,y0+1,z0)
c110 = hash3(x0+1,y0+1,z0)
c001 = hash3(x0,y0,z0+1)
c101 = hash3(x0+1,y0,z0+1)
c011 = hash3(x0,y0+1,z0+1)
c111 = hash3(x0+1,y0+1,z0+1)
Interpolate x:
x00 = lerp(c000,c100,u)
x10 = lerp(c010,c110,u)
x01 = lerp(c001,c101,u)
x11 = lerp(c011,c111,u)
Interpolate y:
y0 = lerp(x00,x10,v)
y1 = lerp(x01,x11,v)
Interpolate z:
lerp(y0,y1,w)
Good.

Let's maybe think about if `camera.updateMatrixWorld()` before raycast is enough. In Three.js, Object3D.updateMatrixWorld updates matrix from position/quaternion/scale if matrixWorldNeedsUpdate or force? It computes local matrix from position/quaternion/scale if `matrixWorldNeedsUpdate`? Actually `updateMatrixWorld(force)` does: if force || matrixWorldNeedsUpdate, then if parent updateMatrixWorld? Let's recall r128: `updateMatrixWorld(force) { if (this.matrixAutoUpdate) this.updateMatrix(); if (this.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate || force) { if (this.parent === null) this.matrixWorld.copy(this.matrix); else this.matrixWorld.multiplyMatrices(this.parent.matrixWorld, this.matrix); this.matrixWorldNeedsUpdate = false; force = true; } const children = this.children; for ... children[i].updateMatrixWorld(force); }` It updates local matrix if matrixAutoUpdate. So camera.updateMatrixWorld() updates. Good.
